## R30H-124W8.5c.2 — increment 6b.3: gated shared Boat E22 airtime admission (2026-11-26)

- Bind the dormant Boat physical worker to `E22AdaptiveAdmission`; shared application/handshake requests now retain owner reply classification and use the adaptive policy. The adapter's legacy direct `admit()` path is disabled while adaptive admission is bound.
- Add a cold-start quiet-history path to `PhysicalWorker`. It requires uninterrupted worker observations for one hour with an initialized, configured E22, no pending TX and no transport fault. Any missing/failed observation restarts the quiet interval. Only after that interval may policy configuration proceed, and only when the supplied profile is commissioned, the explicit airtime-evidence flag is set, and a nonzero RF-tail bound under 60 seconds is supplied. The worker's adaptive adapter remains closed before configuration. Each worker restart begins a new quiet hour because the rolling policy is RAM-resident.
- Existing E22 driver duty buckets remain as a secondary guard. No persistent NVS history, measured RF profile or tail measurement was supplied by this change. Current compile-only config leaves the profile uncommissioned, tail at zero and evidence flag false; it cannot transmit. Legacy production and upload remain locked.
- Local validation: `git diff --check` passed. PlatformIO and C++ compiler are unavailable in this workspace, so physical-worker compile, Boat host tests and lifecycle tests were not run. No CI was triggered because changes remain local.

### W8.4f follow-up — real LINK_PROBE compatibility audit (2026-11-26)

- The mock Remote recovery path sends a tagged `RADIO_MSG_LINK_PROBE` challenge and requires a matching authenticated echo. The real Boat `receiveCaptured` post-session allowlist excludes `RADIO_MSG_LINK_PROBE`; its later branch would return without echo even if reached. The real Remote receive path accepts only an all-zero probe payload and treats it as a no-op. Therefore the mock challenge/echo exchange is not interoperable with either live endpoint and cannot qualify primary recovery.
- Moved the unchanged tagged Challenge/Echo payload codec from the Remote-only mock header into canonical `Common/source/recovery_probe_wire.h`; Remote's recovery gate now includes the staged common header. Manually staged and SHA-256 checked all 37 canonical Common files and manifests for both endpoints. This is a source-sharing change only; no runtime endpoint behavior changed. `git diff --check` passes; tests/compiler remain unavailable.
- Added a Boat challenge handler restricted to the compiled endpoint path with `BOAT_R30H121_PHYSICAL_OWNER`, worker runtime ownership, primary link only, stale-primary condition, a <100 ms captured frame, exact tagged payload/sequence, active session HMAC, and per-link plus cross-link sequence acceptance. It sends a tagged echo through the existing owner TX path at heartbeat priority; STOP/STATUS priority still wins. Probe input does not refresh authenticated link freshness or actuator authority. The existing `BOAT_R30H121_PHYSICAL_OWNER == 0` static lock remains, so this handler is dormant.
- The real Remote parser now accepts a correctly tagged Echo only on primary. Its payload has no command dispatch; like the pre-existing zero-payload LINK_PROBE, an authenticated accepted frame still passes through the generic RX freshness path. It does not by itself rearm motion or bind the mock recovery policy to production. Recovery therefore remains unbound. Endpoint regressions were added in the following subsection; they still need execution in CI, along with worker-disabled verification, before any Remote recovery binding. Production remains Legacy-only; no physical RF or release status changes.

### R30H-124W8.5c.2 — increment 7b: exclusive managed E22 admission (2026-11-26)

- `E22AdaptiveAdmission` now attempts a one-time driver binding. A bound driver is written only through its private managed seam; its direct `sendFrame`/`trySendFrame` route returns unavailable. A second manager is rejected. Drivers with pre-existing legacy airtime charges cannot transfer into a fresh manager, so the new policy cannot forget old budget.
- Managed writes skip the driver's legacy ledger and charge the single adaptive `LoRaRollingLedger` once.
